# Daily video prompt pack — 23/10/2026 · Horse day (ថ្ងៃមមី)

Lucky signs: Horse, Tiger, Dog · Careful: Rat
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Luckiest animal today – Horse (មមី) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color green (បៃតង) · best time 19:00–21:00 · lucky numbers 3 · 73 · 339 · 7302 · 24877 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of a proud, adorable golden horse with big sparkling eyes and a flowing shining mane, the Horse of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The horse symbolizes energy, freedom and success: a free, energetic and joyful expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the horse, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok
A 60-second video about the Horse, today's luckiest animal: 6 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference picture of the Horse as an ingredient. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 6.

### Topic and audience
Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Horse, in one minute. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Horse, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Horse? Today is a Great Day for you. Here's your lucky color, time, and numbers.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមីមែនទេ? ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។
★ 2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Question
   English: Which zodiac animal is the luckiest today? Here's the answer, and what it means for you.
   Khmer: តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ? នេះគឺជាចម្លើយ និងអត្ថន័យសម្រាប់អ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: energetic, independent, and adventurous.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ឆ្អែតថាមពល មានឯករាជ្យ និងចូលចិត្តការងារថ្មី។
5. Fact
   English: Today is a Horse day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Horse today's luckiest sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃមមី ហើយតាមវដ្ដដប់ពីរថ្ងៃ ឆ្នាំមមី គឺជារាសីដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)

A 180-second video about the Horse, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference picture of the Horse as an ingredient. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Horse's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Horse and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
លេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺលេខបី លេខចិតសិបបី និងលេខបីរយសាមសិបប្រាំបួន។ សូមចងចាំលេខទាំងនេះ ដើម្បីរំលឹកខ្លួនឯងថា ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add the 12 character pictures as ingredients (each sign's clips use its own picture). Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Take It Easy
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Ordinary Day
1:20 Tiger (ខាល) – Good Day
1:40 Rabbit (ថោះ) – Ordinary Day
2:00 Dragon (រោង) – Ordinary Day
2:20 Snake (ម្សាញ់) – Ordinary Day
2:40 Horse (មមី) – Great Day

4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Curiosity
   English: Three signs are lucky today, and one should take it easy. Where does your sign stand?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានបីឆ្នាំដែលមានសំណាងល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំដែលគួរសម្រាក។ តើឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នក នៅត្រង់ណា?
2. Question
   English: Which of the twelve zodiac signs is the luckiest today?
   Khmer: តើរាសីណា ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ?
3. Practical
   English: Your lucky color, best time, and direction for today, for all twelve signs, in about five minutes.
   Khmer: ពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ សម្រាប់រាសីទាំង 12 ក្នុងរយៈពេលប្រហែលប្រាំនាទី។
4. Identity
   English: Whatever your sign, today has a lucky color and a best time just for you.
   Khmer: មិនថាអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វីទេ ថ្ងៃនេះមានពណ៌សំណាង និងពេលល្អ សម្រាប់អ្នក។
5. Daily fortune
   English: Today is a Horse day. Here's what that means for every sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃមមី។ មកមើលថា វាមានន័យយ៉ាងណា សម្រាប់រាសីនីមួយៗ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Every viewer has a sign in this video, so this gives everyone a reason to keep watching until their chapter.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Horse and the Silent Temple Bell" (សេះមាស និងកណ្ដឹងប្រាសាទដែលស្ងាត់): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden horse and Kong the little frog must bring the bell's voice back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Kong once (prompt below) and add it and the Horse picture as ingredients in Flow. 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Kong the little frog (make once, keep it)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of Kong, a tiny cute green-and-gold frog with big sparkling eyes, a wide happy smile and a little lotus-leaf hat, inspired by the charm of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. Adorable expressive face, warm magical glow, on a glowing pink lotus by a pond at night, a softly lit Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat and a full moon behind. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Once upon a time
1:20 The lucky color
1:40 The lucky path
2:30 The stone door
3:40 The full-moon festival
4:30 Today's luck

### Topic and audience
An original animated bedtime story starring the Horse, with today's lucky color, direction and numbers in the plot. For families, children and zodiac fans who enjoy gentle stories.

### Opening hook – 5 options

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden horse bring the bell's voice back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the bell had lost its voice. When it was struck, there was only silence. Without its song, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden horse. Today was a Horse day, and the horse felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Horse," he said, "only you can bring the bell's voice back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Free and spirited, the Horse symbolizes energy, freedom, and success on the road ahead. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the golden bell, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the horse, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Horse are energetic, independent, and adventurous. Horses love freedom and new experiences.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was green, the horse's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The horse tied a green ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the northwest, the horse's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path led into a dark, quiet forest. The horse stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Horse can be impatient or struggle to commit long-term.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the horse walked into the forest. Its golden glow lit up the trees, and soon the whole forest was full of light.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Horse today, that is from 7 to 9 in the evening. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the horse rang the golden bells 3 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The horse climbed 73 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a small golden crystal, humming a soft song. It was the heart of the bell!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the crystal, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Monday and Wednesday shone brightest, the Horse's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the crystal carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The horse placed the crystal inside the bell. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, dong! A deep, sweet sound rang across the land!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The bell sang again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The horse felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Horse, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: green. Best time: from 7 to 9 in the evening. Lucky direction: northwest. Lucky numbers: 3, 73, and 339.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ កណ្ដឹងបានបាត់សំឡេង។ ពេលគេវាយ មានតែភាពស្ងាត់ប៉ុណ្ណោះ។ បើគ្មានសំឡេងរបស់វា ពិធីបុណ្យមិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

សេះ មានសេរីភាព និងពោរពេញដោយថាមពល។ ឆ្នាំមមីតំណាងឲ្យកម្លាំង សេរីភាព និងភាពជោគជ័យនៅលើផ្លូវខាងមុខ។ គង់ញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

ដើម្បីដាស់កណ្ដឹងមាស វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រីស្តាល់មាសតូចមួយ កំពុងបន្លឺបទចម្រៀងថ្នមៗ។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃកណ្ដឹង!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគ្រីស្តាល់ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃពុធ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមី។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រីស្តាល់យ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
សេះដាក់គ្រីស្តាល់ចូលក្នុងកណ្ដឹង។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក ដុង! សំឡេងដ៏ពីរោះ បន្លឺឡើងពាសពេញទឹកដី!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ សេះមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមី ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

```
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

72
00:03:03,333 --> 00:03:05,896
សេះឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 73 កាំ

73
00:03:05,946 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

74
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,652
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

75
00:03:11,702 --> 00:03:15,056
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រីស្តាល់មាសតូចមួយ

76
00:03:15,106 --> 00:03:17,610
កំពុងបន្លឺបទចម្រៀងថ្នមៗ។

77
00:03:17,660 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃកណ្ដឹង!

78
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,200
នៅជុំវិញគ្រីស្តាល់

79
00:03:21,250 --> 00:03:23,700
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

80
00:03:23,750 --> 00:03:25,992
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

81
00:03:26,042 --> 00:03:28,075
ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃពុធ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

82
00:03:28,125 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំមមី។

83
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,600
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

84
00:03:31,650 --> 00:03:35,678
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រីស្តាល់យ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

85
00:03:35,728 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

86
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

87
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

88
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

89
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,941
សេះដាក់គ្រីស្តាល់ចូលក្នុងកណ្ដឹង។

90
00:03:52,991 --> 00:03:55,557
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

91
00:03:55,607 --> 00:03:56,866
បន្ទាប់មក ដុង!

92
00:03:56,916 --> 00:03:59,950
សំឡេងដ៏ពីរោះ បន្លឺឡើងពាសពេញទឹកដី!

93
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,677
កណ្ដឹងច្រៀងម្ដងទៀត។

94
00:04:01,727 --> 00:04:02,950
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

95
00:04:03,000 --> 00:04:06,495
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

96
00:04:06,545 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

97
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

98
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

99
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
សេះមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

100
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

101
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមី ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

102
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

103
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

104
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,671
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

105
00:04:31,721 --> 00:04:33,147
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

106
00:04:33,197 --> 00:04:35,524
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 យប់។

107
00:04:35,574 --> 00:04:37,163
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសពាយព្យ។

108
00:04:37,213 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 3 លេខ 73 និងលេខ 339។

109
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

110
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

111
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

112
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

113
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

114
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

115
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

116
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-23-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:01,715
Can one little golden horse

2
00:00:01,765 --> 00:00:03,479
bring the bell's voice back

3
00:00:03,529 --> 00:00:05,244
before the full moon rises?

4
00:00:05,294 --> 00:00:07,989
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

5
00:00:08,039 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

6
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,181
But this morning

7
00:00:11,231 --> 00:00:13,335
the bell had lost its voice.

8
00:00:13,385 --> 00:00:14,719
When it was struck

9
00:00:14,769 --> 00:00:16,488
there was only silence.

10
00:00:16,538 --> 00:00:17,719
Without its song

11
00:00:17,769 --> 00:00:19,950
the festival could not begin.

12
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,749
In the middle of the pond

13
00:00:21,799 --> 00:00:23,259
a golden lotus opened

14
00:00:23,309 --> 00:00:25,777
and out came a little golden horse.

15
00:00:25,827 --> 00:00:27,288
Today was a Horse day

16
00:00:27,338 --> 00:00:29,950
and the horse felt something special.

17
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,389
A little frog named

18
00:00:31,439 --> 00:00:33,132
Kong came hopping over.

19
00:00:33,182 --> 00:00:34,874
"Little Horse," he said

20
00:00:34,924 --> 00:00:38,056
"only you can bring the bell's voice back.

21
00:00:38,106 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

22
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,182
Free and spirited

23
00:00:41,232 --> 00:00:43,138
the Horse symbolizes energy

24
00:00:43,188 --> 00:00:45,964
freedom, and success on the road ahead.

25
00:00:46,014 --> 00:00:46,834
Kong smiled.

26
00:00:46,884 --> 00:00:48,283
That was exactly why

27
00:00:48,333 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

28
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,819
To wake the golden bell, the little

29
00:00:52,869 --> 00:00:55,606
hero needed three lucky treasures:

30
00:00:55,656 --> 00:00:58,147
its lucky color, its lucky path

31
00:00:58,197 --> 00:00:59,950
and its lucky numbers.

32
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:03,009
"Let's go!" said the horse

33
00:01:03,059 --> 00:01:06,656
and it set off on its adventure

34
00:01:06,706 --> 00:01:09,950
with Kong right by its side.

35
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,283
People born in the Year of the Horse are

36
00:01:13,333 --> 00:01:16,617
energetic, independent, and adventurous.

37
00:01:16,667 --> 00:01:19,950
Horses love freedom and new experiences.

38
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,206
The first treasure was waiting

39
00:01:22,256 --> 00:01:24,837
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

40
00:01:24,887 --> 00:01:26,717
Only one shone brightest.

41
00:01:26,767 --> 00:01:28,597
It was green, the horse's

42
00:01:28,647 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

43
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,880
The horse tied a green

44
00:01:31,930 --> 00:01:33,897
ribbon around its neck.

45
00:01:33,947 --> 00:01:36,880
"Wear your lucky color," said Kong

46
00:01:36,930 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

47
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,430
Next, they came to a crossroads

48
00:01:42,480 --> 00:01:43,710
with many paths.

49
00:01:43,760 --> 00:01:45,550
One path began to glow.

50
00:01:45,600 --> 00:01:47,710
It pointed to the northwest

51
00:01:47,760 --> 00:01:49,950
the horse's lucky direction.

52
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,305
But the path led into

53
00:01:51,355 --> 00:01:52,660
a dark, quiet forest.

54
00:01:52,710 --> 00:01:53,821
The horse stopped.

55
00:01:53,871 --> 00:01:55,240
Sometimes, people born

56
00:01:55,290 --> 00:01:57,885
in the Year of the Horse can be impatient

57
00:01:57,935 --> 00:01:59,950
or struggle to commit long-term.

58
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,546
Kong came close and smiled.

59
00:02:02,596 --> 00:02:05,046
"Breathe slowly," he said.

60
00:02:05,096 --> 00:02:06,777
"Trust your heart.

61
00:02:06,827 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

62
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,820
Step by step, the horse

63
00:02:11,870 --> 00:02:13,690
walked into the forest.

64
00:02:13,740 --> 00:02:17,104
Its golden glow lit up the trees, and soon

65
00:02:17,154 --> 00:02:19,950
the whole forest was full of light.

66
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

67
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

68
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

69
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

70
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

71
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

72
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

73
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,537
"The door opens only

74
00:02:41,587 --> 00:02:43,918
at your best time," said Kong.

75
00:02:43,968 --> 00:02:45,426
For the Horse today

76
00:02:45,476 --> 00:02:48,204
that is from 7 to 9 in the evening.

77
00:02:48,254 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

78
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,502
When the time came

79
00:02:51,552 --> 00:02:54,864
the horse rang the golden bells 3 times

80
00:02:54,914 --> 00:02:56,847
its first lucky number.

81
00:02:56,897 --> 00:02:57,881
Ding!

82
00:02:57,931 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

83
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,086
The door opened

84
00:03:01,136 --> 00:03:03,586
and a stairway of light appeared.

85
00:03:03,636 --> 00:03:06,162
The horse climbed 73 glowing steps

86
00:03:06,212 --> 00:03:07,905
its second lucky number

87
00:03:07,955 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

88
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,140
At the top of the tower

89
00:03:12,190 --> 00:03:15,283
they found a small golden crystal

90
00:03:15,333 --> 00:03:17,188
humming a soft song.

91
00:03:17,238 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the bell!

92
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,797
Around the crystal, seven little lights

93
00:03:22,847 --> 00:03:25,643
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

94
00:03:25,693 --> 00:03:28,271
Monday and Wednesday shone brightest

95
00:03:28,321 --> 00:03:29,950
the Horse's lucky days.

96
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,969
Time was running out.

97
00:03:32,019 --> 00:03:34,758
Holding the crystal carefully

98
00:03:34,808 --> 00:03:37,450
the little hero hurried home

99
00:03:37,500 --> 00:03:39,950
with Kong right beside it.

100
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

101
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

102
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

103
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

104
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,138
The horse placed the crystal

105
00:03:52,188 --> 00:03:53,388
inside the bell.

106
00:03:53,438 --> 00:03:55,809
For a moment, nothing happened.

107
00:03:55,859 --> 00:03:56,747
Then, dong!

108
00:03:56,797 --> 00:03:59,950
A deep, sweet sound rang across the land!

109
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,589
The bell sang again.

110
00:04:01,639 --> 00:04:03,720
Lanterns rose into the sky

111
00:04:03,770 --> 00:04:06,425
and the full-moon festival began.

112
00:04:06,475 --> 00:04:07,737
Everyone cheered

113
00:04:07,787 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

114
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,669
The full moon shone down warmly

115
00:04:12,719 --> 00:04:14,687
as if to say thank you.

116
00:04:14,737 --> 00:04:17,669
The horse felt peaceful and proud.

117
00:04:17,719 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

118
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,311
If you were born in the Year of the Horse

119
00:04:23,361 --> 00:04:25,114
today is your day too.

120
00:04:25,164 --> 00:04:27,737
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

121
00:04:27,787 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

122
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,922
Let's remember today's luck.

123
00:04:31,972 --> 00:04:33,260
Lucky color: green.

124
00:04:33,310 --> 00:04:35,936
Best time: from 7 to 9 in the evening.

125
00:04:35,986 --> 00:04:37,837
Lucky direction: northwest.

126
00:04:37,887 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 3, 73, and 339.

127
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

128
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Kong fell

129
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

130
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

131
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

132
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

133
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

134
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

135
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Scene 1 – Horse day
Magical and powerful. A proud, adorable golden horse with big sparkling eyes and a flowing shining mane stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it rears up as golden wings of light unfold from its sides, then gallops into the sky across the moon. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. A proud, adorable golden horse with big sparkling eyes and a flowing shining mane, a powerful yet adorable golden tiger cub with big bright eyes and glowing engraved stripes and a loyal, adorable golden puppy with big shining eyes and a jeweled collar fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Rat, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. An adorable golden rat with big sparkling eyes, tiny jade-green ear jewels and a proud little smile curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The rat matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)
